## User-directed increment 2026-10-13 — Negative resistance weaknesses

- **Completed:** per-type reductions now accept finite values in −100000 to +100000. A negative value adds its magnitude to that active incoming channel before general Armor is subtracted once. A channel with zero incoming damage stays zero, preventing unrelated weaknesses or empty hits from manufacturing damage. Incoming weapon/attack damage and general Armor remain nonnegative; invalid/nonfinite/out-of-bound profiles fail closed.
- **Files changed:** `Source/KalmalaGameplay/Public/KalmalaDamageProfile.h`, `Source/KalmalaGameplay/Private/KalmalaDamageProfile.cpp`, `Source/KalmalaGameplay/Private/Tests/KalmalaDamageProfileTest.cpp`, `docs/01-game-design.md`, `docs/02-technical-architecture.md`, `docs/05-decision-log.md`, `docs/07-development-setup.md`, `BACKLOG.md`, and this entry.
- **Lightweight checks:** Python checked negative and positive resistance arithmetic for all eight channels, absent/empty-channel suppression, armor ordering (10 slash with −5 slash reduction and 2 Armor → 13 damage), unchanged positive mixed-hit result (59), and source enforcement of signed bounds versus nonnegative incoming damage/Armor. Source inspection confirmed the common resolver is still used by both server health paths and that Hearth Shield receives resolved damage. `git diff --check` and current-run MAX_PATH checks passed. Unreal regressions were updated for weaknesses, inactive channels, excessive negatives and nonfinite values but not executed.
- **Impact/authority:** entity authors can define weaknesses by setting the matching reduction below zero; current zero-default balance remains unchanged. The common resolver remains server-side, without new client setters, requests, replication or saved fields.
- **Verification/limitations:** normal user-directed increment, not milestone-final. Full Unreal/build/automation/runtime verification remains deferred. Weaknesses are flat additions, applied once per active channel per hit; general Armor remains nonnegative. Pre-existing `Scripts/Render-Menu-folder.lnk`, `KalmalaGameMode.cpp`, and `KalmalaInteractionGrid.h` changes were preserved and excluded.

## User-directed increment 2026-10-13 — Typed weapon bonuses and flat entity defenses

- **Completed:** migrated all 13 tool profiles to schema-7 per-level `DamageBonuses` (Slash/Stab/Crush/Pierce/Fire/Water/Holy/Poison) plus bounded unique `Attacks` with additive style bonuses. Removed scalar CombatDamage and exclusive DamageType authoring/accessors. Bronze Axe remains 50/55 slash bonus; melee spears become stab and picks crush while preserving damage totals. The existing server-selected Basic attack combines item/style bonuses and strength before sending a typed hit. Flat per-channel resistance is clamped independently, then general Armor is subtracted once; default defenses are all zero. Resistance components are defined on the eight production gameplay entity classes: player, wildlife, construction, campfire, harvest node, discovery, hazard, and skiff. Player/wildlife health mutations use resolved server damage, with Hearth Shield afterward; defeat feedback reads post-hit health. Inventory/crafting show all active item channels and the Forge uses slash-bonus wording.

## Autonomous increment 2026-10-18 — River hydrology-grid seam regression

- **Completed:** added `Kalmala.World.Regional.RiverHydrologyGridSeam`. Bounded generated-segment searches require actual river crossings of both X and Y hydrology-grid boundaries for seeds 418 and 419. ## User-directed increment 2026-10-18 — Ban portable construction kits

- **Completed:** all 13 player-placed objects now resolve to direct-material Building Hammer recipes. Converted the remaining Chest, Cooking Rack, Cauldron and Frying Pan routes; Frying Pan keeps its 5 Iron cost and no longer has a Forge crafting step. The loader separates the 12 placed-object metadata records from inventory Items into Buildables (Campfire already had no inventory definition). Runtime now loads 46 inventory definitions instead of 58; all existing placement identities/icons remain usable as metadata. Every Buildables entry must have a direct-material recipe, and the placement supported-set is the single direct-build supported-set. Removed the one-kit placement cost fallback completely. Existing Hammer ownership, terrain, payment, persistence, raw hearth fuel and station-attachment validation remain server-owned.
- **Permanent prevention:** inventory catalogue validation rejects supported construction identities and unknown Kit-suffixed IDs in Items; FindItem/IsValidStack/CanAddToStack and server grant/transaction/storage/save paths cannot admit a construction object. Ordinary recipe scaling cannot manufacture buildables, even through a forged kit-output recipe. FindBuildable/FindDefinition are explicit presentation lookups used by station/construction UI. AGENTS.md now forbids portable construction items, production/loot/grant/storage paths and any future kit fallback. Old development kit stacks are rejected, not converted; no save schema or migration added. Legacy internal Kit-suffixed actor/icon IDs remain placement metadata, not inventory items.
- **Files:** AGENTS.md, BACKLOG.md, PROGRESS.md; Content/Data/GameCatalogues/recipes/building.json; Scripts/Tests/Construction/Verify-Crafting.ps1 and Scripts/Tests/Survival/Verify-PersistedCampRestart.ps1; gameplay KalmalaCraftingComponent, KalmalaCraftingVerification, KalmalaGameCatalogueLoader, KalmalaItemCatalogue(.h/.cpp), KalmalaPlacementPreview, KalmalaRecipeCatalogue, KalmalaStorageInteraction, KalmalaStorageVerification; existing gameplay tests CookingHeatContract, Crafting, ItemCatalogue, M9GrindingStoneRepair, M9RaisedStorage and M9ToolProgressionCatalogue; new KalmalaDirectBuildTest.cpp; UI CraftingSubsystem, ItemDetailWidget, RecipeRequirements, StationContextWidget and existing InteractionPrompt/RecipeBrowse/StatusHotbar tests; docs/02-technical-architecture.md, docs/05-decision-log.md and docs/07-development-setup.md.
